- [ ] **Step 3: Model additions**

In `OBAKit/Mapping/Layers/MapSheetView.swift`, add above `MapSheetModel`:

```swift
/// One tile of the layers grid (spec 3.9).
struct MapLayerTile: Identifiable, Equatable {
    let id: String
    let title: String
    let iconName: String
    let isEnabled: Bool
    let unavailableReason: String?
    let group: MapLayerGroup

    /// Unavailable blocks turning a layer on; an enabled layer is always switchable off.
    var isTapEnabled: Bool { unavailableReason == nil || isEnabled }

    var subtitle: String {
        if let unavailableReason { return unavailableReason }
        return isEnabled ? Strings.mapLayersStateOn : Strings.mapLayersStateOff
    }
}
```

and inside `MapSheetModel`, after `setEnabled(_:layer:)`:

```swift
    func tiles(in group: MapLayerGroup) -> [MapLayerTile] {
        visibleLayers(in: group).map { layer in
            let reason: String? = {
                if case .unavailable(let reason) = layer.availability { return reason }
                return nil
            }()
            return MapLayerTile(id: layer.id, title: layer.title, iconName: layer.iconName, isEnabled: isEnabled(layer), unavailableReason: reason, group: layer.group)
        }
    }

    func toggle(_ tile: MapLayerTile) {
        guard let layer = mapRegionManager.mapLayer(id: tile.id) else { return }
        setEnabled(!tile.isEnabled, layer: layer)
    }

    /// The group, header included, disappears when no rental layer is registered.
    var showsRentalsGroup: Bool { !visibleLayers(in: .otherModes).isEmpty }

    /// Chips show whenever the group does, so the range can be set before a layer is on.
    var showsRangeChips: Bool { showsRentalsGroup }
```

`MapLayerGroup` must be `Equatable` for `MapLayerTile`'s synthesized conformance; it is a plain enum, so it already is.

- [ ] **Step 4: Tile and chip views**

Create `OBAKit/Mapping/Layers/MapLayerTileView.swift`:

```swift
//
//  MapLayerTileView.swift
//  OBAKit
//
//  Copyright © Open Transit Software Foundation
//  This source code is licensed under the Apache 2.0 license found in the
//  LICENSE file in the root directory of this source tree.
//

import OBAKitCore
import SwiftUI

/// A 62 pt layer tile: icon well, title, On/Off (or the unavailable reason).
/// On, it takes its group's tint; unavailable, it dims to 0.5.
struct MapLayerTileView: View {
    let tile: MapLayerTile
    let tint: Color
    let onTap: () -> Void

    private static let height: CGFloat = 62
    private static let cornerRadius: CGFloat = 16
    private static let wellSize: CGFloat = 34
    private static let offWell = Color(red: 0xe9 / 255.0, green: 0xe9 / 255.0, blue: 0xee / 255.0)

    var body: some View {
        Button(action: onTap) {
            HStack(spacing: 10) {
                ZStack {
                    Circle().fill(tile.isEnabled ? Color.white.opacity(0.22) : Self.offWell)
                    Image(systemName: tile.iconName).font(.body.weight(.semibold))
                }
                .frame(width: Self.wellSize, height: Self.wellSize)
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 1) {
                    Text(tile.title).font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold)).lineLimit(1)
                    Text(tile.subtitle).font(.caption).lineLimit(1)
                }
                Spacer(minLength: 0)
            }
            .padding(.horizontal, 12)
            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: Self.height, alignment: .leading)
            .foregroundStyle(tile.isEnabled ? Color.white : Color.primary)
            .background(tile.isEnabled ? tint : Color(uiColor: .systemBackground), in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: Self.cornerRadius, style: .continuous))
        }
        .buttonStyle(.plain)
        .disabled(!tile.isTapEnabled)
        .opacity(tile.unavailableReason == nil ? 1 : 0.5)
        .accessibilityLabel(tile.title)
        .accessibilityValue(tile.subtitle)
        .accessibilityAddTraits(tile.isEnabled ? .isSelected : [])
    }
}

/// The "Min. range" chips shown under the Rentals group.
struct RentalRangeChipRow: View {
    let presets: [RentalRangePreset]
    let selectedID: Int
    let onSelect: (Int) -> Void

    var body: some View {
        HStack(spacing: 8) {
            Label(Strings.mapLayersMinRange, systemImage: "bolt")
                .font(.footnote.weight(.semibold))
                .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
            ForEach(presets) { preset in
                let isSelected = preset.id == selectedID
                Button(preset.title) { onSelect(preset.id) }
                    .font(.footnote.weight(.semibold))
                    .padding(.horizontal, 10)
                    .padding(.vertical, 6)
                    .foregroundStyle(isSelected ? Color.white : Color.primary)
                    .background(isSelected ? Color(uiColor: .rentalPurple) : Color(uiColor: .systemBackground), in: Capsule())
                    .accessibilityAddTraits(isSelected ? .isSelected : [])
            }
            Spacer(minLength: 0)
        }
    }
}
```

    private static let sheetBackground = Color(uiColor: .systemGroupedBackground)
    private static let gridSpacing: CGFloat = 12

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            ScrollView {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 20) {
                    basemapPicker
                    layerGroup(title: Strings.mapLayersGroupTransit, group: .transit, tint: Color(uiColor: ThemeColors.shared.brandAccent))
                    if model.showsRentalsGroup {
                        layerGroup(title: Strings.mapLayersGroupRentals, group: .otherModes, tint: Color(uiColor: .rentalPurple))
                    }
                    if model.showsRangeChips {
                        RentalRangeChipRow(presets: model.rangeFilterPresets, selectedID: model.selectedRangePresetID) { model.selectRangePreset(id: $0) }
                    }
                    pointsOfInterestCard
                }
                .padding(16)
            }
            .background(Self.sheetBackground)
            .navigationTitle(OBALoc("map_sheet.title", value: "Map", comment: "Title of the Map sheet"))
            .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItem(placement: .topBarLeading) {
                    if model.showsResetButton {
                        Button(OBALoc("map_sheet.reset", value: "Reset", comment: "Button restoring the default map layer configuration")) {
                            model.resetToDefaults()
                        }
                    }
                }
                ToolbarItem(placement: .topBarTrailing) {
                    Button(Strings.done) { dismiss() }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: - Basemap

    private var basemapPicker: some View {
        Picker(selection: Binding(get: { model.selectedBaseType }, set: { model.selectBaseType($0) })) {
            Label(OBALoc("map_sheet.basemap_standard", value: "Standard", comment: "Basemap style: standard street map"), systemImage: "map").tag(MapBaseType.standard)
            Label(OBALoc("map_sheet.basemap_satellite", value: "Satellite", comment: "Basemap style: satellite imagery"), systemImage: "globe.americas.fill").tag(MapBaseType.satellite)
            Label(OBALoc("map_sheet.basemap_hybrid", value: "Hybrid", comment: "Basemap style: satellite imagery with labels"), systemImage: "map.fill").tag(MapBaseType.hybrid)
        } label: {
            EmptyView()
        }
        .pickerStyle(.segmented)
    }

    // MARK: - Layer groups

    @ViewBuilder
    private func layerGroup(title: String, group: MapLayerGroup, tint: Color) -> some View {
        let tiles = model.tiles(in: group)
        if !tiles.isEmpty {
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
                Text(title).font(.footnote.weight(.semibold)).foregroundStyle(.secondary).textCase(.uppercase)
                LazyVGrid(columns: [GridItem(.flexible(), spacing: Self.gridSpacing), GridItem(.flexible(), spacing: Self.gridSpacing)], spacing: Self.gridSpacing) {
                    ForEach(tiles) { tile in
                        MapLayerTileView(tile: tile, tint: tint) { model.toggle(tile) }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: - Points of Interest

    private var pointsOfInterestCard: some View {
        Toggle(isOn: Binding(get: { model.showsPointsOfInterest }, set: { model.setShowsPointsOfInterest($0) })) {
            Label(
                OBALoc("map_sheet.shows_points_of_interest", value: "Points of Interest", comment: "Map sheet toggle for Apple MapKit Points of Interest (restaurants, shops, etc.)"),
                systemImage: "mappin.and.ellipse"
            )
        }
        .padding(16)
        .background(Color(uiColor: .systemBackground), in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 16, style: .continuous))
    }

import Foundation

/// The DRT copy catalogue (spec 2.8) is in the English table and its plurals resolve.
@MainActor
@Suite(.serialized)
final class OnDemandStringsTests {

    private static let keys = [
        "on_demand.card.eyebrow", "on_demand.card.call_to_book", "on_demand.card.details", "on_demand.card.more_services",
        "on_demand.status.open_until", "on_demand.status.open_now_until", "on_demand.status.open", "on_demand.status.opens", "on_demand.status.book_by", "on_demand.status.closed",
        "on_demand.tag.same_day", "on_demand.tag.advance", "on_demand.tag.no_notice", "on_demand.tag.eligibility",
        "on_demand.bar.inside", "on_demand.bar.badge", "on_demand.bar.a11y.show_edge", "on_demand.bar.a11y.zoom_out", "on_demand.bar.a11y.call", "on_demand.bar.a11y.more_services",
        "on_demand.picker.title", "on_demand.picker.title_nearby", "on_demand.picker.subtitle_location", "on_demand.picker.subtitle_center", "on_demand.picker.subtitle_point",
        "on_demand.picker.your_location", "on_demand.picker.map_center", "on_demand.picker.selected_place", "on_demand.picker.footer",
        "on_demand.detail.location_inside", "on_demand.detail.location_outside", "on_demand.detail.center_inside", "on_demand.detail.center_outside",
        "on_demand.detail.point_inside", "on_demand.detail.point_outside", "on_demand.detail.location_sub", "on_demand.detail.includes_location",
        "on_demand.detail.where_header", "on_demand.detail.service_area", "on_demand.detail.drop_off", "on_demand.detail.zone_count", "on_demand.detail.no_service", "on_demand.detail.open_agency_website",
        "on_demand.planner.section", "on_demand.planner.serves_both", "on_demand.planner.caption", "on_demand.planner.show_all", "on_demand.planner.empty", "on_demand.planner.status_now",
        "on_demand.address.inside", "on_demand.address.inside_more", "on_demand.address.outside",
        "map_layers.group.transit", "map_layers.group.rentals", "map_layers.state.on", "map_layers.state.off", "map_layers.min_range"
    ] + CompassDirection.allCases.flatMap { ["on_demand.bar.inside_near_edge.\($0.rawValue)", "on_demand.bar.outside.\($0.rawValue)"] }

    private var english: [String: String] {
        let bundle = Bundle(for: DonationCell.self)
        let url = bundle.url(forResource: "Localizable", withExtension: "strings", subdirectory: nil, localization: "en")!
        return NSDictionary(contentsOf: url) as! [String: String]
    }

    @Test func `Every on-demand key is in the English table`() {
        let table = english
        for key in Self.keys {
            #expect(table[key] != nil, key)
        }
    }

    @Test func `Plural keys resolve one and other forms`() {
        #expect(OnDemandCopy.moreServices(1) == "1 more service here")
        #expect(OnDemandCopy.moreServices(3) == "3 more services here")
        #expect(OnDemandCopy.pickerTitle(count: 1, nearby: false) == "1 service here")
        #expect(OnDemandCopy.pickerTitle(count: 2, nearby: true) == "2 services nearby")
        #expect(OnDemandCopy.zoneCount(1) == "1 zone")
        #expect(OnDemandCopy.zoneCount(4) == "4 zones")
        #expect(OnDemandCopy.addressInsideMore(name: "Dial-a-Ride", extra: 1) == "Inside Dial-a-Ride and 1 more")
    }
}
```

The plural test assumes the test host runs in English; `LocalizationTests` already makes that assumption for its `rental_cluster.title_fmt` check.

- [ ] **Step 4: Translations for the twelve other locales**

`LocalizationTests` fails the build when any locale lacks a key `en` has. Every non-English `Localizable.strings` and `Localizable.stringsdict` (`ar`, `es`, `fil`, `fr`, `it`, `ko`, `pl`, `pt-BR`, `ru`, `vi`, `zh-Hans`, `zh-Hant`) needs every key above with a real translation — the previous on-demand strings commit (`1ec0cf11`, "Translate the on-demand strings") is the precedent, and the review there rejected English copy in other locales. Produce them with the `localizer` skill:

```
/localizer Translate the new on_demand.* and map_layers.* keys just appended to OBAKit/Strings/en.lproj/Localizable.strings and the five new plural entries in OBAKit/Strings/en.lproj/Localizable.stringsdict into every other OBAKit/Strings/*.lproj Localizable.strings and Localizable.stringsdict (ar, es, fil, fr, it, ko, pl, pt-BR, ru, vi, zh-Hans, zh-Hant). Keep every format specifier (%@, %d, %1$@, %2$d, %#@count@) identical, keep the /* comment */ line above each entry, append in the same order as the English file, and use the existing on_demand.* translations in each file as the terminology reference (e.g. the French "Réserver en ligne" for booking). For the stringsdict, add every CLDR plural category the locale's existing entries use (Polish and Russian use few/many; Arabic uses zero/two/few/many).
```

Then verify with the parity tests below; a locale the skill leaves incomplete fails `Every locale has same keys as english` with the missing keys named.

```

Expected: PASS (`Every locale has same keys as english`, `Every locale preserves english format specifiers`, `Every locale stringsdict is well formed`, and the plural forms).

### Task 18: Manual exercise against local maglev

No code. Everything below is run and observed; each check is a line in the task's report with pass/fail and, on failure, the shortest reproduction.

**Files:**
- Test: none (manual). Reads spec §6.3.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the whole feature; maglev on branch `gtfs-flex` with `configs/flex-charlevoix.json`.
- Produces: the report.

- [ ] **Step 1: Start the server and point the app at it**

```bash
cd ~/repos/onebusaway/maglev && make build && ./bin/maglev -f configs/flex-charlevoix.json
```

Expected: the server listens on port 4124 (`curl -s 'http://localhost:4124/api/ondemand/services-for-location.json?lat=45.3&lon=-85.2&radius=5000&geometryDetail=none&key=test' | head -c 300` prints a `code":200` envelope with `CC_CC1`). If the app's requests come back 401, add the key the simulator build sends (`org.onebusaway.iphone` — see `OBAKitTests/Helpers/OBATestCase.swift:88` for the shape) to the config's `api-keys`.

Build and run the app, then add the region with the deep link and move the simulated location into Charlevoix County:

```bash
cd /Users/aaron/repos/onebusaway/.worktrees/ios-gtfs-flex
scripts/generate_project OneBusAway
xcodebuildmcp simulator build-and-run --project-path OBAKit.xcodeproj --scheme App --simulator-id 2E39F22F-CD68-4233-A01E-4662F9F16196
xcrun simctl openurl 2E39F22F-CD68-4233-A01E-4662F9F16196 'onebusaway://add-region?name=Charlevoix%20Local&oba-url=http%3A%2F%2Flocalhost%3A4124%2F'
xcrun simctl location 2E39F22F-CD68-4233-A01E-4662F9F16196 set 45.3,-85.2
```

Expected: the app switches to "Charlevoix Local" and the map centres on the simulated location.

- [ ] **Step 2: Region level (spec §6.3 checks 1–2)**

Zoom out until the county fits. Expected: the Charlevoix services draw as polygons with one labelled pin each, every pin inside its own polygon (Dial-a-Ride, Medical Trips, Beaver Island; the Ironton Ferry stop group has no polygon and no pin); the zone card appears with the top service and "N more services here" matching the number of other services containing the point.

- [ ] **Step 3: Street level (check 3)**

Zoom in past the stop gate. Expected: pins vanish, strokes become 4 pt with a halo, the bar appears with page dots and a "1 of N" badge; swiping a page highlights that service's stroke; tapping the thumbnail zooms back out to region level.

- [ ] **Step 4: Near-edge and outside (checks 4–6)**

```bash
xcrun simctl location 2E39F22F-CD68-4233-A01E-4662F9F16196 set 45.3739,-85.0      # ~50 m inside the county's north edge
xcrun simctl location 2E39F22F-CD68-4233-A01E-4662F9F16196 set 45.3785,-85.0      # ~500 m outside
xcrun simctl location 2E39F22F-CD68-4233-A01E-4662F9F16196 set 45.43,-85.0        # ~6 km outside
```

Expected, in order: "Inside · edge <distance> north" with a feet/metre distance; the bar turns gray, reads "<distance> north of the zone", and its chevron pans the map to the boundary; the bar disappears. (Adjust the latitudes if the county boundary in the feed sits elsewhere: read the `bbox` in the probe response.)

- [ ] **Step 5: Picker and detail (checks 7–8)**

Move back inside (`set 45.3,-85.2`), long-press the bar. Expected: the picker lists every match in tier order with a "Boyne City · Your location"-style subtitle (or "Your location" if geocoding is slow). Open each detail: tags; a status line for Dial-a-Ride (same-day) or a promoted "Book by …" row for an advance service; the location row; the thumbnail with the blue dot; Where (Medical Trips only), When with a "Sun · No service" row for Mon–Sat services, How to book. Open the stop `CC_CC_Ironton_Ferry_East` from search: its on-demand card lists the Ironton Ferry service and the page opens without a location row.

- [ ] **Step 6: Address check and planner (checks 9–10, classic shell)**

Long-press the map inside the county and again outside. Expected: the callout reads "Inside Charlevoix County Dial-a-Ride" / "Outside …". With trip planning on for the region (Settings), plan a trip between two points inside the county with no fixed route. Expected: the "On-demand options" card appears above the planner sheet with Dial-a-Ride and a Call to Book pill; a plan that finds a bus route shows no card.

- [ ] **Step 7: Layers sheet and clock (checks 11–12)**

Open the layers sheet: tiles for Transit stops and On-demand zones; no Rentals group in this region; toggle On-demand zones off. Expected: polygons and the dock vanish, a long-pressed pin still reads Inside/Outside, Reset appears. Turn it back on. With a tier-1 service on page 1, set the simulator clock past `runningUntil` (16:40 agency time; `xcrun simctl status_bar … ` does not move the clock — change the Mac's clock, or use the `OnDemandProbeControllerTests` boundary test as the automated stand-in). Expected: within 2 s the title changes from "Pickups available here" to "Opens …".

- [ ] **Step 8: Regular width and the other shell (check 13, spec 2.10)**

Run on an iPad simulator (`xcrun simctl list devices | grep iPad`) in landscape. Expected: the dock sits above the side panel at its width, never spans the map, and the control stack is not overlapped. Then enable Settings → Experimental → map panel experience and repeat Steps 2, 3, 5 and 7 in the panel shell (no long-press and no planner there).

- [ ] **Step 9: Manistee and an unsupported server**

If `~/repos/onebusaway/maglev/configs/flex-manistee.json` exists, restart maglev with it and repeat Steps 2, 3, 5 and 6 near Manistee: the zone-to-zone service (MC2) shows the Where section with Drop-off, and the planner fallback qualifies only rules in the right direction. Finally switch the app's region to Puget Sound (a stock OBA server without `/api/ondemand`). Expected: nothing new appears anywhere and the layers sheet has no On-demand zones tile.

- [ ] **Step 10: Report**

Write the thirteen checks with pass/fail in the task report. No commit.
